# B14 Executable-Evidence Role Manifest

## Authority

This materialized manifest is part of the read-only B14 audit. Its runtime,
executable-evidence, and recipe authority is the fixed tree:

```text
micros commit: da0bd95148f2704d5347c2af4c64085b90132746
micros tree:   3b90c7716d69efd68930b3f8ab86a32813bdd6c3
```

The current audit-document branch was verified to have no executable-source,
recipe, production, or public-header difference from that pin. The 43
configure presets were regenerated before graph extraction. CMake/Ninja graph
nodes, active per-target compiler dependency records and definitions, CTest
JSON inventory, Python imports, actual preprocessor test-condition expressions,
and explicit production bridges form the pinned closure.

W01/W44 run the same Python discovery. A targeted trace binds every tracked
file read or metadata check to both workflows. Five documentation/process
fixtures were not already present in pinned recipe/import/compiler closure;
they are recorded as additional live current-tree rows because three changed
after the runtime pin. Their contents are not runtime-baseline authority.

Counts:

- primary B14 paths: **636**;
- native CTests: **35**;
- registered native test workflows: **7**;
- QEMU workflows: **38**;
- standalone-image workflows: **4**;
- complete registered evidence workflows: **49**;
- authoritative full-tier workflows: **43**;
- exact selected compile contexts: **169**;
- tracked W01/W44 Python fixture/data access paths: **1035**;
- additional live Python fixture/data rows: **5**, with **3** changed from the runtime pin;
- paths containing an actual test-build preprocessor conditional: **63**; and
- complete secondary evidence-role rows: **1063**.

Every primary B14 path has at least one CTest or workflow consumer. Every row
has pinned recipe/import/conditional/bridge authority or explicit live-fixture
authority. Header consumers and compile contexts come only from active
selected-object compiler dependency records; inactive preprocessor branches
are not propagated as consumers.

## Role codes

| Code | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `primary` | Primary executable-evidence ownership assigned to B14 |
| `recipe` | CMake/preset/toolchain recipe authority |
| `conditional` | A preprocessor conditional expression selects a `MICROS_*TEST` macro |
| `bridge` | Explicit in-production test bridge |
| `ctest` | Selected by one or more native CTest executables |
| `workflow` | Selected by one or more registered evidence workflows |
| `import` | Imported support for a selected Python oracle/runner |
| `fixture` | Tracked file read or inspected by W01/W44 Python discovery |
